################################
# Grand Offensive
################################
event = {
	id = 1300002
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	#Need to have tag, otherwise it won't show in the logs
	TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U37 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }
	
	decision = {
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { local_flag = grand_offensive }
	}

	decision_trigger = {
	}

	trigger = {
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { local_flag = grand_offensive }
		NOT = { local_flag = partisan_mode   }
		
		#majors should not waste it on non-majors
		OR = {
			major = no
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = VIC }
			war = { country = FRA }
			war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = USA }
			war = { country = ITA }
			war = { country = SOV }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				#Every winter on the anniversary of the revolution :X, until the motherland is freed
				country = SOV
				supplies = 5000
				lost_VP = { value = 5 }
				month = November
				day = 5 #+5 offset around the right date
			}
			AND = {
				country = GER
				supplies = 5000
				OR = {
					#Once at Fall gelb
					AND = {
						war = { country = VIC }
						war = { country = BEL }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					#Landed in England
					AND = {
						flag = ENG_invaded
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
					}
					#Every summer while at war with the Soviets
					AND = {
						war = { country = SOV }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
					#Germany invaded, need to expel the enemy ASAP
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = VIC
				supplies = 3000
				OR = {
					#Fall gelb (regardless of when Germany chose to start it) and every summer afterwards until the motherland is freed
					AND = {
						war = { country = GER }
						alliance = { country = BEL }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = ITA
				supplies = 3000
				OR = {
					#Once at war entry, once at operation sunflower, and every summer if invaded
					AND = {
						alliance = { country = GER }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						alliance = { country = GER }
						event = 2405100 #afrika korps
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
						random = 20
					}
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = ENG
				supplies = 3000
				OR = {
					#Once for husky and once (twice if no husky) for operation overlord/dragoon
					AND = {	
						event = 2405060
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						OR = {
							event = 2405040
							event = 2405050
						}
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
					}
					#Also once if enemy landed, and every summer afterwards until the motherland is freed
					AND = {
						flag = ENG_invaded
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						flag = ENG_invaded
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = USA
				supplies = 3000
				OR = {
					#Once for husky and once (twice if no husky) for operation overlord/dragoon
					AND = {	
						event = 2405060
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						OR = {
							event = 2405040
							event = 2405050
						}
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
					}
					#Also once if enemy landed, and every summer afterwards until the motherland is freed
					AND = {
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = JAP
				supplies = 3000
				OR = {
					#Once after Marco Polo, once at the start of the Pacific War/Soviet War, and every summer if invaded
					AND = {
						war = { country = CHI }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {
						OR = {
							war = { country = USA }
							war = { country = ENG }
						}
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
					}
					AND = {
						war = { country = SOV }
						NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV } }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = CHI
				supplies = 1500
				OR = {
					#Once after Marco Polo, and every summer if not at AsiaPassive until the motherland is freed
					AND = {
						war = { country = JAP }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						NOT = { local_flag = asiaPassive }
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = CHC
				supplies = 300
				OR = {
					#Once at long march, once at 100 regiments offensive, and every summer if not at AsiaPassive until the motherland is freed
					AND = {
						war = { country = CHI }
						local_flag = { which = longMarch_count when = 1 value = 1 }
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {
						war = { country = JAP }
						event = 1330110170
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 2 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						NOT = { local_flag = asiaPassive }
						OR = {
							event = 1330110170
							year = 1942
						}
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = SPA
				supplies = 500
				OR = {
					AND = {
						war = { country = SPR }
						event = 2401602 #Treaty of munich
						#repeatable, until SPR is destroyed
					}
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 30 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				country = SPR
				supplies = 500
				OR = {
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 35 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				#all other countries
				NOT = {
					ispuppet = yes
					major = yes
					country = CHI
					country = CHC
					country = VIC
					country = SPR
					country = SPA
				}
				supplies = 500
				OR = {
					#Immediately if invaded or under attack, and then every summer until the motherland is freed
					AND = {	
						OR = {
							lost_national = { value = 5 }
							AND = {
								under_attack = -1
								country = FIN
							}
						}
						NOT = { local_flag = { which = grand_offensiveCount when = 1 value = 1 } }
					}
					AND = {	
						lost_national = { value = 5 }
						month = May
						NOT = { month = October }
						random = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = "GrandOffensiveName"
	desc = "GrandOffensiveDesc" 
	style = 2
	picture = "Wehrmacht"
	decision_picture = "decision_grand_offensive"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 4
	deathdate = { day = 30 month = december year = 1999 }

	action = {
		name = "GrandOffensiveText"
		command = { type = local_setflag 						which = grand_offensive }
		command = { type = local_setflag when = 1				which = grand_offensiveCount value = 1 }

		
		#increased supply consumption will cause increase in TC demand, this will partially cover it
		command = { type = tc_mod 											value = 100	}
		command = { type = supply_dist_mod 									value = 10	}
		
		#the cost is relative to military size.
		command = { type = supply_consumption 	which = land 				value = 1	}
		command = { type = supply_consumption 	which = air 				value = 1	}
		command = { type = supply_consumption 	which = naval				value = 0.5	}
		command = { type = fuel_consumption		which = land 				value = 0.2	}
		command = { type = fuel_consumption 	which = air 				value = 0.2	}
		command = { type = fuel_consumption 	which = naval				value = 0.2	}
		
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = attack 					value = 0.2	}
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = support_attack 			value = 0.2	}
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = support_defense			value = 0.1	}
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = planned_defense			value = 0.1	}
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = reserves				value = 0.1	}
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = airborne_assault 		value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = amphibious_assault 		value = 0.1 }
		
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Air_superiority 		value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Air_scramble 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Ground_attack 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = interdiction 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = convoy_air_raiding 		value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Naval_strike 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Strategic_bombardment 	value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Logistical_strike 		value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Air_supply 				value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Port_strike 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Installation_strike 	value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Runway_cratering 		value = 0.1 }
		
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = convoy_raiding 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = asw 					value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = naval_interdiction 		value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = shore_bombardment 		value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = sea_transport 			value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = naval_combat_patrol 	value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = sneak_move 				value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = naval_scramble 			value = 0.1 }
		
		command = { type = ground_def_eff 	when  = 1						value = 0.05 }
		
		command = { type = morale 			which = land 					value = 10	}
		command = { type = morale 			which = naval 					value = 10	}
		command = { type = morale 			which = air 					value = 10	}
		
		command = { type = speed 			which = land 					value = 2	}
		command = { type = counterattack 									value = 2	}
		command = { type = assault 											value = 8	}
		command = { type = ambush 											value = 2	}
		command = { type = delay 											value = -2	}
		command = { type = tactical_withdrawal 								value = -2  }
		command = { type = breakthrough 									value = 2	}
		command = { type = encirclement 									value = 2	}
		command = { type = hq_supply_eff 									value = 2	}
		command = { type = sce_frequency 									value = 0.3	}
		command = { type = attrition_mod 									value = 2	}
		command = { type = trickleback_mod 									value = 5	}
		
		command = { type = ai 						 which = "offensiveSupplyEnable.ai" }
		
		command = { type = event which = 1300003 where = -1 				when = 30   }
		command = { type = event which = 1300004 where = -1 				when = 180	} #6 months
	}
}

event = {
	id = 1300003
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	#Need to have tag, otherwise it won't show in the logs
	TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U37 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }

	name = "GrandOffensiveOverName"
	desc = "GrandOffensiveDesc" 
	style = 2
	picture = "Wehrmacht"

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK

		#the cost is relative to military size.
		command = { type = supply_consumption 	which = land 				value = -1	 }
		command = { type = supply_consumption 	which = air 				value = -1	 }
		command = { type = supply_consumption 	which = naval				value = -0.5 }
		command = { type = fuel_consumption		which = land 				value = -0.2 }
		command = { type = fuel_consumption 	which = air 				value = -0.2 }
		command = { type = fuel_consumption 	which = naval				value = -0.2 }

		command = { type = tc_mod 											value = -100 }
		command = { type = supply_dist_mod 									value = -10	 }
		
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = attack 					value = -0.2 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = support_attack 			value = -0.2 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = support_defense			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = planned_defense			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency	which = reserves				value = -0.1 }

		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Air_scramble 			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Ground_attack 			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = interdiction 			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = convoy_air_raiding 		value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Naval_strike 			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Strategic_bombardment 	value = -0.1 }

		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Air_supply 				value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Port_strike 			value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = Installation_strike 	value = -0.1 }

		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = convoy_raiding 			value = -0.1 }

		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = naval_interdiction 		value = -0.1 }
		command = { type = task_efficiency 	which = shore_bombardment 		value = -0.1 }

		command = { type = morale 			which = naval 					value = -10	 }

		command = { type = speed 			which = land 					value = -2	 }
		command = { type = counterattack 									value = -2	 }

		command = { type = ai 						 which = "offensiveSupplyDisable.ai" }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 1300004
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	#Invisible reset
	
	name = EVT_1300004_NAME
	desc = EVT_1300004_DESC 
	style = 2
	picture = "Wehrmacht"

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK
		trigger = { local_flag = grand_offensive }
		
		command = { type = local_clrflag 						which = grand_offensive }
	}
}

################################
# Equipment decisions
################################
event = { #start winterequip production
	id = 9000000
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U37 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }

	decision = {
		technology = 5920	# Infantry equipment - winter
		technology = 5930	# Machinery adaption - winter
		NOT = { local_flag = wequip }
	}

	decision_trigger = {
		technology = 5130	# Assembly Line Experimentation
	}

	trigger = {	
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ai = yes
				OR = {
					technology = 5920	# Infantry equipment - winter
					technology = 5930	# Machinery adaption - winter
				}
			}
			AND = {
				ai = no
				technology = 5920	# Infantry equipment - winter
				technology = 5930	# Machinery adaption - winter
				technology = 5130	# Assembly Line Experimentation
			}
		}
		NOT = { local_flag = wequip }
		OR = { country = FIN country = U20 country = SOV country = RUS country = SIB country = SWE country = CAN country = SCA }
		NOT = {	
			OR = {
				resource_shortage = supplies
				resource_shortage = oil
			}	
		}	
	}

	name = 9000000_name
	desc = 9000000_desc
	style = 2

	id = 9000020

	persistent = yes

		NOT = { local_flag = issue_currency }
		local_flag = AllInit
	}

	}

		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = {
			local_flag = issue_currency
			atwar   = yes
			money   = 10
			dissent = 3
		}
	}
   
	name = "9000022name"
	desc = "9000022desc" 
	style = 2
	picture = "issue_currency"
	decision_picture = "decision_issue_currency"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 180
	deathdate = { day = 30 month = december year = 1999 }

	action = {
		name = "9000022text"
		command = { trigger = { IC = 200 } 						type = money value = 6400 }
		command = { trigger = { IC = 100 NOT = { IC = 200 } }	type = money value = 3200 }
		command = { trigger = { IC = 50 NOT = { IC = 100 } }	type = money value = 1600 }
		command = { trigger = { IC = 25 NOT = { IC = 50 } }		type = money value = 800  }
		command = { trigger = { IC = 12 NOT = { IC = 25 } }		type = money value = 400  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { IC = 12 } } 			type = money value = 200  }
		
		#Eventually need to transition to a multiplier model rather than fixed tiers. Possible solution: move the CG to money modifier from the war_level slider itself to the corresponding event, that way it should be ignored in calculations?
		#command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = exchange_rate 						} } 		type = resource				which = money	value = 30000  }
		#command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 20	} } 		type = resource				which = money	value = 10000  }
		
		#Each monney issue devaluates the local currency, making it less valuable. To compensate and get the same amount in 'international dollars', more local currency has to be printed each time.
		#Theoretically devaluation should also make local investments cheaper as well, but this is difficult to implement.
		
		command = { 																					type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -3   } #1 is temp
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 1 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 2 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 3 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 4 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 5 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 6 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 7 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 8 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 9 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 10 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 11 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 12 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = exchange_rate when = 1 value = 13 	} }			type = industrial_modifier 	which = IC 		value = -0.2 }

		command = { 																					type = industrial_modifier	which = money	value = -3	 }

		#Each WARDebt is equivalent to 0.5 GDP, so 4 WARDebt is 2 GDP. Effectively this is almost same as "invest in growth", except this will:
		#Take effect in December rather than in a month
		#Reduce possible interest payments that would normally happen in December, in this sense it may be a better long term option compared to "invest in growth"
	}
#	In theory this should not be necessary since supplies can typically be traded for money on the market, however:

		command = { trigger = { IC = 12 NOT = { IC = 25 } }		type = money value = -400  }

	  	command = { trigger = { OR	= { technology = { country = USA value = 4650 } technology = { country = GER value = 4650 } } } type = add_division value = tactical_bomber when = 10 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = invest_tacObs_count when = 1 value = 1 }

event = {
	id = 9000051

	persistent = yes
	random = no

	decision = { 
		NOT = { local_flag = transports_conversion }
		#atwar 		= yes
		convoypool 	= 100
	}
	decision_trigger = { 
		#Will trigger a financial crisis if you go negative
		money = 200
	}
	trigger = { 
		NOT = { local_flag = transports_conversion }
		OR = {
			atwar 	= yes
			random	= 10
		}
		convoypool 	= 300
		money		= 200
		OR = {
			AND = {
				navy = 1
				NOT = { transport = { value = 1 } }
			}
			AND = {
				navy = 50
				NOT = { transport = { value = 2 } }
			}
			AND = {
				navy = 100
				NOT = { transport = { value = 3 } }
			}
			AND = {
				local_flag = AI1936_mixed
				NOT = { transport = { value = 6 } }
			}
			AND = {
				local_flag = AI1936_naval
				NOT = { transport = { value = 12 } }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "9000051name"
	desc = "9000051desc"
	style = 2
	picture = "Convoys"

#### Cannibilizing divisions when low on manpower (AI only)
#when – 0 - scrap most obsolete or damaged units first. where = 0 - return remaining MP to the pool. Garrisons and militia are usually prioritized.
event = {
	id = 9000061
	#make invisible later
	TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U37 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }
	persistent = yes
	random = no

	trigger = { 
		ai = yes
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { manpower = 20 }
		losses = 100000 #suffering losses
		army = 2
		OR = {
			policy = { type = professional_army value = 10 }
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 9 }
				ispuppet = yes
			}
			#AND = {
			#	policy = { type = professional_army value = 5 }
			#	NOT = {
			#		local_flag = puppetLevel7
			#		local_flag = puppetLevel8
			#		local_flag = puppetLevel9
			#		local_flag = legitIndie
			#	}
			#}
		}
	}

	name = EVT_9000061_NAME
	desc = EVT_9000061_DESC
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 30 month = december year = 1999 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_9000061_ACTA
		ai_chance = 5
		trigger = { army = 3 }

		command = { type = demobilize which = land when = 0 where = 0 value = -1 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = cannibilize_division_count when = 1 value = 1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_9000061_ACTB
		ai_chance = 5
		trigger = { army = 4 }

		command = { type = demobilize which = land when = 0 where = 0 value = -2 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = cannibilize_division_count when = 1 value = 2 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_9000061_ACTC
		ai_chance = 90
		trigger = { army = 5 }

		command = { type = demobilize which = land when = 0 where = 0 value = -3 }

	}

event = {
	id = 9000080
	random = no
	persistent = yes

	decision = { 
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = partisan_mode 	}
		NOT = { local_flag = partisan_chg	}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { ispuppet = yes } #puppets can't partisan mode unless by event
		
		#Can't switch to partisan mode right after a long and bloody conventional war. Need to wait for the long war counter to go down.
		OR = {
			NOT = { local_flag = { which = warDurationCount when = 1 value = 4 } } #4 years of war
			NOT = { local_flag = { which = mpRelatLCount 	when = 1 value = 4 } } #8 mil total losses at China tier
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = partisan_mode	}
		NOT = { local_flag = partisan_chg	}
		NOT = { local_flag = partisan_stop	}
		
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { ispuppet = yes }
		OR = {
			NOT = { local_flag = { which = warDurationCount when = 1 value = 4 } } #4 years of war
			NOT = { local_flag = { which = mpRelatLCount 	when = 1 value = 4 } } #8 mil total losses at China tier
		}
		OR = {
			local_flag = AllDefenseSpecWinter
			local_flag = AllDefenseSpecMountain
			local_flag = AllDefenseSpecJungle
			#local_flag = AllDefenseSpecDesert
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				OR = { country = CHI country = CHC }
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 45 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT 	= { IC = 20    }
				lost_IC = { value = 70 }
			}
		}
		manpower = 200
	}
   
	name = EVT_9000080_NAME
	desc = EVT_9000080_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "sov_partisans"
	decision_picture = "decision_grand_offensive3"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 30 month = december year = 1999 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_9000080_ACTA
		trigger = { NOT = { country = CHI country = CHC } }
		#This is never a popular choice, especially since it means populated areas are being abandoned to enemy occupation without a fight
		command = { 																	type = dissent value = 10 } 
		command = { trigger = { OR = { IC = 20 NOT = { lost_IC = { value = 70 } } } }	type = dissent value = 10 } 
		command = { trigger = { OR = { IC = 40 NOT = { lost_IC = { value = 50 } } } }	type = dissent value = 10 } 
		command = { trigger = { OR = { IC = 60 NOT = { lost_IC = { value = 30 } } } }	type = dissent value = 10 } 
		command = { trigger = { OR = { IC = 80 NOT = { lost_IC = { value = 10 } } } }	type = dissent value = 10 } 
		command = { type = local_setflag which = partisan_mode_init }
		command = { type = event which = 9000081 where = -1 when = 60 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_9000080_ACTA
		trigger = { OR = { country = CHI country = CHC } }
		#This is never a popular choice, especially since it means populated areas are being abandoned to enemy occupation without a fight
		command = { trigger = { flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 50 } } 		type = dissent value = 5 } 
		command = { trigger = { flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 45 } } 		type = dissent value = 5 }

#
#	From Nick's mod: forced resource trades in case Allies are trying to starve Axis nations pre-war.
#	Default resource ratios below. Actual cost will be 120% relative to normal, so it is still not a great trade for the Axis.
#	supplies-coal	1-8
#	supplies-metal	1-8
#	supplies-rare	1-2
#	supplies-oil	1-2.5
#	supplies-money	3-1
#########################################################################
#  Gold trade with USA
#########################################################################
event = {
	id = 4440653
	random = no
	persistent = yes

	decision = { 
		NOT = { local_flag = MPTradeGold }
		OR = {
			major = yes
			country = VIC
		}
		NOT = { country = USA }
		NOT = { 
			ai = yes
			ai = USA
			war = { country = USA }
			war = { country = ENG }
			local_flag = Sanctions
		}
	}
	trigger = { 	
		ai = no
    }

	name = EVT_4440653_NAME
	desc = EVT_4440653_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "Credit_flow"
	decision_picture = "decision_money_devaluation"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 1 month = april year = 1939 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_4440653_ACTA
		command = { type = local_setflag which = MPTradeGold }
		#normal is -15
		command = { trigger = { country = ENG } type = trade which = ENG where = USA supplies = -18 money = 5 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = FRA } type = trade which = FRA where = USA supplies = -18 money = 5 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = GER } type = trade which = GER where = USA supplies = -18 money = 5 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = JAP } type = trade which = JAP where = USA supplies = -18 money = 5 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = ITA } type = trade which = ITA where = USA supplies = -18 money = 5 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = SOV } type = trade which = SOV where = USA supplies = -18 money = 5 when = 540 value = 1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_4440653_ACTB
		command = { type = local_setflag which = MPTradeGold }
		#normal is -30
		command = { trigger = { country = ENG } type = trade which = ENG where = USA supplies = -36 money = 10 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = FRA } type = trade which = FRA where = USA supplies = -36 money = 10 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = GER } type = trade which = GER where = USA supplies = -36 money = 10 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = JAP } type = trade which = JAP where = USA supplies = -36 money = 10 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = ITA } type = trade which = ITA where = USA supplies = -36 money = 10 when = 540 value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = SOV } type = trade which = SOV where = USA supplies = -36 money = 10 when = 540 value = 1 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 4440660
	random = no
	persistent = yes

	trigger = { 
		OR = { 
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = USA }
			local_flag = Sanctions
		}
		OR = { 
			local_flag = MPTradeGold
			local_flag = MPTradeOil
			local_flag = MPTradeRare
		}
		OR = {
			trade = { country = ENG }
			trade = { country = USA }
		}
	}

	name = EVT_4440660_NAME
	desc = EVT_4440660_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "Credit_flow"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 1 month = april year = 1939 }

		name = ACTION_NAME_DAMN

		command = { type = embargo which = USA where = -1 value = 1  }
		command = { type = embargo which = ENG where = -1 value = -1 }
		command = { type = embargo which = USA where = -1 value = -1 }

		command = { type = local_clrflag which = MPTradeOil  }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = MPTradeRare }
	}
}
